## Realistic Spring Greeting Card Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
STRICTLY preserve the appearance of the woman from the photo 1:1 — face, features, proportions, age, skin texture, and individual characteristics. Do not change or stylize the face.
Create a realistic spring greeting card in the format of a photograph.
A woman is standing outdoors among the first spring flowers. Her face is in a slight three-quarter profile, with a soft, lively smile. Her hair falls freely and naturally.
She is wearing a light spring coat or jacket in a soft yellow palette and a light scarf. In her hands is a fresh bouquet of crocuses and hyacinths.
Background: blurred trees with the first buds, soft green grass, and sunny spring light. A light breeze creates a sense of movement.
Lighting is natural — soft daytime sun, warm highlights on her hair and skin. Colors are fresh and lively.
The composition is designed as a printed greeting card:
— a neat light frame
— light bokeh
— soft depth of field
In the upper part of the greeting card, written in a beautiful, readable font:
"{argument name="greeting text" default="Happy First Day of Spring!"}"
Photorealism, high detail, the atmosphere of a genuine spring moment.
```

## Natural Travel Lifestyle Snapshot

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"overall_aesthetic": "Natural Travel Lifestyle / Mediterranean Vacation Snapshot",

  "scene_context": {
    "story": "Two close friends relaxing together during a summer vacation on a boat, captured as a natural travel memory rather than a styled photoshoot.",
    "intent": "The image should feel like a spontaneous holiday photo taken during a trip, not a fashion or editorial shoot."
  },

  "environment": {
    "location": "Open sea setting on a wooden boat platform with netted lounging area",
    "water": "Clear turquoise Mediterranean sea, calm surface, bright daylight reflections",
    "structure": "Wooden beams, rope net platform, soft white pillows, relaxed travel atmosphere"
  },

  "lighting": {
    "type": "Natural daylight",
    "quality": "Soft, clean sunlight with natural shadows",
    "effects": "Realistic outdoor lighting without cinematic or stylized effects"
  },
  
  "composition": {
    "shot_type": "Casual wide shot",
    "camera_style": "Handheld travel photo",
    "framing": "Unposed, natural framing as if taken by a friend during a trip",
    "depth_of_field": "Natural depth, no artificial blur or cinematic focus"
  },

  "subjects": {
    "dynamic": "Friendly, relaxed, and natural interaction",
    "relationship": "Clearly portrayed as friends, not romantic or intimate",
    "body_language": "Casual resting poses, relaxed posture, natural expressions, no stylized posing",
    "emotion": "Comfortable, joyful, calm vacation mood"
  },

  "fashion_and_styling": {
    "clothing": "Simple summer swimwear suitable for vacation and beach activities",
    "style": "Practical, casual holiday outfits rather than styled fashion looks",
    "grooming": "Natural hair, minimal makeup, realistic travel appearance"
  },

  "mood_and_atmosphere": {
    "vibe": "Carefree, friendly, and relaxed summer holiday energy",
    "description": "Feels like a real travel memory from a summer trip, focused on friendship, relaxation, and vacation atmosphere rather than aesthetics or visual styling."
  },

  "safety_context": {
    "classification": "travel_lifestyle",
    "non_sexualized": true,
    "non_editorial": true,
    "non_fetishized": true,
    "friendship_focused": true
  }
}
```

## Ultra-Realistic Outdoor Shower Portrait Prompt (JSON)

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "subject": "Beautiful young woman woman, mid 20s, voluptuous yet toned feminine physique, light-to-medium warm beige skin tone with soft peach undertones. Natural skin texture with visible pores and slightly wet surface with subtle reflective sheen. Soft oval face, gently rounded cheeks with natural blush, smooth feminine jawline, rounded chin. Almond-shaped medium brown eyes with calm, confident expression, slightly hooded eyelids, natural lashes. Medium-thickness softly arched dark brown eyebrows. Medium straight nose with softly rounded tip. Full natural pink lips with warm undertone and light gloss. Dark brown hair, slicked-back wet look, center-parted, smooth strands pulled behind shoulders. Wearing a deep royal blue textured triangle bikini top with thin neck-tie straps and matching low-rise bikini bottoms, slightly shimmering when wet.",

  "composition": "Mid-shot at eye level, framing from upper thighs to just above the head. Subject standing directly beneath a wall-mounted metal shower spout positioned immediately above and slightly behind her right shoulder. The shower fixture is physically close to the subject and clearly visible beside her head and upper torso. Water stream falling onto her upper body. Subject and shower spout occupy the same focal plane with no background separation blur.",

  "environment": "Private outdoor stone shower courtyard. Large gray stone slab walls with visible cracks, weathering, and damp stains. Wet stone tile floor with reflective surface directly beneath subject. Potted green plant in a brown clay pot near a simple wooden spa bench along the wall.",

  "lighting_geometry": "Natural outdoor directional sunlight from above and slightly to the side, illuminating both subject and shower fixture equally. Clear highlights on wet skin and metallic shower spout. Water droplets catching light with crisp specular reflections. Soft shadow gradients across stone wall.",

  "materials_and_texture": "Visible natural skin pores and moisture sheen; defined water droplets and thin continuous water stream; reflective metal shower spout with sharp edges and clear surface highlights; rough weathered stone walls with cracks and damp staining; wet tile reflections beneath subject; textured bikini fabric with subtle shimmer.",

  "camera_optics": "50mm lens, eye-level angle, natural perspective. Moderate depth of field (equivalent to f/5.6–f/8). Subject, shower spout, and water stream all in sharp focus with no background blur.",

  "color_strategy": "Cool neutral stone tones contrasted with deep royal blue bikini. Warm beige skin tones provide balance. Natural green plant adds subtle organic contrast. Realistic color grading with true-to-life skin rendering.",

  "rendering_intent": "Ultra-realistic photorealistic rendering, editorial lifestyle photography aesthetic, physically accurate lighting
```

## Lo-Fi Analog Boudoir 2x2 Grid Contact Sheet

> Minimal, lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "lofi_analog_boudoir_red_satin_grid_2x2_no_text",
      "language": "en",
      "priority": "highest",
      "version": "v1.0_LOFI_RED_SATIN_BOUdoir_2X2_FILMROLL"
    },
    "input": {
      "mode": "image_to_image",
      "reference_image_usage": "maximum",
      "preserve_identity": true,
      "preserve_facial_features": true,
      "preserve_hairstyle": true,
      "preserve_wardrobe": true,
      "notes": "Use the reference as the primary style anchor. Create a 2x2 grid with the SAME adult woman identity and the SAME red satin dress across all panels. Lo-fi analog film look, heavy grain, soft focus, slight motion blur from movement, intimate boudoir mood. Background alternates between deep red wall and a gray curtain/doorway edge. No text, no logos, no watermark."
    },
    "output_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "1:1",
      "resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
      "num_images": 1,
      "layout": {
        "type": "grid",
        "rows": 2,
        "cols": 2,
        "gutter": "thin",
        "outer_border": "none",
        "panel_consistency": "high"
      },
      "sharpness": "soft_film",
      "grain": "heavy_analog",
      "render_style": "lofi_intimate_film"
    },
    "scene": {
      "concept": "analog film boudoir contact sheet",
      "environment": "minimal interior, deep red wall + gray curtain/doorframe edge, low light",
      "lighting": {
        "style": "dim warm practical light",
        "key_light": "soft warm side light with gentle falloff",
        "fill": "minimal",
        "avoid": "flash, HDR, crisp modern beauty lighting"
      },
      "camera": {
        "lens": "50mm",
        "look": "handheld film snapshot",
        "focus": "intentionally soft; allow slight motion blur",
        "motion_blur_rule": "blur allowed on hair and face edges only, never melt anatomy"
      }
    },
    "subject": {
      "type": "adult woman",
      "wardrobe": "deep red satin slip dress with draped neckline and thin straps, subtle side slit, tied back detail, no logos",
      "hair": "dark hair with soft fringe/bangs, slightly messy",
      "makeup": "minimal, natural lips, soft eyeliner",
      "anatomy_rules": "realistic proportions, correct hands and fingers, no warped torso"
    },
    "panel_direction": {
      "global_rule": "same identity + same dress; each panel a different pose; keep intimate, elegant body language; avoid explicit nudity",
      "panel_01": "soft motion blur portrait, head tilted, shoulder strap slightly off-shoulder, dreamy expression",
      "panel_02": "standing 3/4 by gray curtain/doorway, arms crossed lightly over chest, subtle gaze",
      "panel_03": "against deep red wall, head thrown slightly back, hand on collarbone, satin highlights visible",
      "panel_04": "tight crop of the dress back/side detail showing tied straps and satin folds (no explicit body exposure)"
```

## Editorial Surrealist Portrait: The Ink Lament

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "vibe_title_en": "The Ink Lament",
  "master_prompt": "A hyper-realistic, surreal editorial portrait of The Melancholic Prodigy, captured in a dimly lit, dust-filled antique letterpress workshop. The protagonist is framed strictly according to the Cinematic Rule of Thirds, positioned on the far right, leaving vast, moody negative space to the left. The subject exhibits a 'sorrowful focus,' staring past the lens. SURREAL VISUAL TRANSLATION: mirroring the dense, swarming geometry of the reference image, the subject's face and neck are not covered in spiders, but in viscous, anti-gravity droplets of black India ink and broken, antique calligraphy nibs that cling to the skin like organic parasites. These elements cast realistic, micro-shadows on the pores, treating the surrealism as a practical effect. The lighting is a high-contrast Chiaroscuro: a single, warm shaft of light cuts through the airborne dust particles, illuminating the glossy texture of the ink against the matte skin, while the rest falls into deep shadow. Shot on a Hasselblad H6D-100c with an HC 100mm f/2.2 lens to achieve a razor-thin depth of field, focusing intensely on the eyes and the tactile ink texture. Film simulation: Kodak Portra 800, rich grain, desaturated slightly to emphasize the melancholic atmosphere. No neon, no digital gloss.",
  "meta": {
    "intent": "Editorial Surrealism",
    "priorities": "Texture, Mood, Narrative Conflict",
    "device_profile": "Hasselblad H6D-100c"
  },
  "frame": {
    "aspect": "16:9",
    "composition": "Rule of Thirds",
    "layout": "Off-Center Negative Space",
    "camera_angle": "Eye Level",
    "tilt_roll_degrees": "0"
  },
  "subject": {
    "gender": "Female",
    "identity": "The Melancholic Prodigy",
    "demographics": "Adult, Ageless",
    "face": "Sorrowful, Intense Focus, Hyper-detailed Pores",
    "hair": "Pulled back or styled simply to focus on face texture",
    "body": "Shoulder-up Portrait",
    "expression": "Stoic Melancholy, Lost in thought",
    "pose": "Static but tense, head slightly turned to the negative space"
  },
  "wardrobe_accessories": {
    "garments": [
      {
        "item": "Vintage Work Smock",
        "material": "Linen",
        "color": "Faded Charcoal",
        "fit": "Loose, Textured"
      }
    ],
    "accessories": [
      {
        "item": "Surreal Ink Droplets",
        "color": "Obsidian Black",
        "material": "Liquid/Viscous",
        "brand_style": "Surreal Organic"
      },
      {
        "item": "Calligraphy Nibs",
        "color": "Rusted Steel",
        "material": "Metal",
        "brand_style": "Antique"
      }
    ]
  },
  "environment": {
    "setting": "Antique Letterpress Workshop",
    "surfaces": "Aged Wood, Dust Motes, Paper Stacks",
    "depth": "Deep shadowy background with bokeh highlights",
    "atmosphere": "Heavy, Dusty, Melancholic, Warm",
    "lens_interaction": "Sharp focus on ink texture, r"

## Flash Photography Aesthetic Prompt with ControlNet

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
"subject": "Young woman, long brown hair, white ribbed tank top with lace trim. Deep scoop neck, visible cleavage, large bust volume with fabric tension. Layered gold necklaces with heart pendant, hoop earrings, gold bracelet. Dark brown patterned shoulder bag.",
"pose": "Leaning against dark surface. Right arm extended out of frame. Left arm angled down. Head tilted slightly right.",
"environment": "Interior. Dark green door or wall with handle on right. Black subway tiles on left.",
"camera": "Eye-level, mid-shot, slight wide-angle perspective.",
"lighting": "Direct, harsh on-camera front flash. Hard drop shadows behind subject.",
"mood_and_expression": "Playful, bright smile, tongue slightly visible.",
"style_and_realism": "Casual snapshot, flash photography aesthetic, raw and unedited.",
"colors_and_tone": "High contrast. Warm flushed skin, bright white top, dark green door, black tiles, brown bag.",
"quality_and_technical_details": "Standard digital flash quality, sharp foreground, slight image noise.",
"aspect_ratio_and_output": "3:4",
"controlnet": {
"pose_control": {
"model_type": "openpose_full",
"purpose": "Strictly lock skeletal alignment, anatomical proportions, torso orientation, and prevent structural or volumetric alteration",
"constraints": "No limb length adjustment, no torso compression, no chest volume reduction, no posture change, no symmetry correction, no ribcage narrowing",
"recommended_weight": 1.2
},
"depth_control": {
"model_type": "depth_midas",
"purpose": "Enforce exact volumetric projection, silhouette area equivalence, curvature depth integrity, and spatial layering",
"constraints": "No depth flattening, no volumetric shrinkage, no perspective reinterpretation, no shadow softening, no foreground-background collapse, no artificial depth of field",
"recommended_weight": 1.25
}
},
"negative_prompt": "stylization, cinematic look, cinematic lighting, cinematic color grading, film look, film simulation, dramatic lighting, HDR effect, tonal remapping, teal and orange grading, DSLR simulation, portrait mode effect, bokeh, background blur, depth of field, lens blur, gaussian blur, soft focus, motion blur, atmospheric haze, volumetric light beams, glow effect, bloom, post-processing effects, AI enhancement, detail enhancement filters, artificial sharpening, oversharpening, skin smoothing, retouching, face enhancement, aesthetic correction, body normalization, shape refinement, geometric reinterpretation, anatomical distortion, breast reduction, volume compression, torso slimming, ribcage narrowing, symmetry correction, proportion changes, depth flattening, shadow softening, beautification filters, perspective alteration"
}
```

```
A very realistic photograph of a person sitting on a motorcycle, seen from the front. The person wears a formal suit, tie and a vintage helmet, his face shows great happiness. The wind blows visibly against their face and suit, creating a dynamic sensation of movement, as if they were traveling at high speed. The overall scene should be on a vibrant yellow background, capturing the realism of a person enjoying a motorcycle ride, with a clear focus on facial expression and the effect of the wind. Captured with the Canon EOS R5 camera with high resolution and ultra-realistic details
```

## Golden Hour Portrait of Man and Cat

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a portrait without altering facial features. Close-up: a man tenderly embraces a large ginger cat. Warm sunset lighting creates deep shadows and golden highlights on the faces. The man has a soft smile and expressive eyes. The cat looks directly into the camera with a majestic air. The atmosphere is cozy, homey, and full of golden hour aesthetics. Highly detailed fur and skin texture, softly out-of-focus background. Golden hour sunlight, dramatic shadows, cozy home atmosphere, cinematic lighting, high resolution, 8K, detailed fur.
```

## Cinematic short video prompt for female portraiture

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A 30-45 second short film depicting the delicate cuteness of a woman in a cinematic style, using warm, soft natural light and shallow depth of field. The composition unfolds in four seamless cuts: a sudden moment of inspiration, a smooth tracking shot moving an object, ASMR-like close-ups capturing the texture of fabric and small items, and finally, a satisfied smile after completing the task. The sound design emphasizes diegetic environmental sounds and breathing, creating a sense of presence through both visual and auditory elements.
```

## iPhone Aesthetic Prompt Generator Framework

> Fotogerçekçi, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
<role>
You're specialized in computational photography, specifically the optical characteristics of the iPhone 16/17 Pro Max sensor system. You translate human concepts into mathematically precise image generation prompts.
</role>

<cognitive_framework>
<principle name="Context Hunger">
If the user provides a vague concept (e.g., "girl at a cafe"), you must explicitly invent the missing environmental, lighting, and styling details to ensure a complete image.
</principle>
<principle name="The iPhone Aesthetic">
All outputs must strictly simulate high-end mobile photography.
- Focal Lengths: 24mm (Main), 13mm (Ultra Wide), or 77mm (Telephoto).
- Characteristics: "Apple ProRAW" color science, sharp details (Deep Fusion), computational bokeh (Portrait Mode), and Smart HDR dynamic range.
- Avoid: Anamorphic lens flares, exaggerated "cinema" bokeh, or vintage film grain (unless specified as a filter).
</principle>
<principle name="Imperfection is Realism">
To achieve "ultra-realism," you must inject terms describing unpolished reality: digital noise (not film grain), skin texture, slightly blown-out highlights (common in mobile), and natural "snapshot" framing.
</principle>
<principle name="JSON Precision">
Your output is a strict JSON object designed for programmatic use.
</principle>
</cognitive_framework>

<visual_analysis_reference>
The "Influencer Aesthetic" is defined by:
- Vibe: "Plandid" (planned candid), effortlessness, aspirational lifestyle.
- Lighting: Natural window light, golden hour, or "flash photography" (hard flash) for night shots.
- Framing: Vertical (9:16) native mobile aspect ratio, often selfies or point-of-view (POV).
</visual_analysis_reference>

<instructions>
1. Analyze the user's request for subject and mood.
2. Enrich the request using "iPhone Photography" constraints.
3. Format the output strictly as a JSON object with the following schema.
</instructions>

<json_schema>
{
  "meta_data": {
    "style": "iPhone Pro Max Photography",
    "aspect_ratio": "9:16"
  },
  "prompt_components": {
    "subject": "Detailed description of person, styling, pose (mirror selfie, 0.5x angle, etc.)",
    "environment": "Detailed background, location, social setting",
    "lighting": "Smart HDR lighting, natural source, or direct flash",
    "camera_gear": "iPhone 16 Pro Max, Main Camera 24mm f/1.78, or Ultra Wide 13mm",
    "processing": "Apple ProRAW, Deep Fusion, Shot on iPhone",
    "imperfections": "Digital noise, motion blur, authentic skin texture, screen reflection (if mirror)"
  },
  "full_prompt_string": "The combined, comma-separated string optimized for realistic mobile generation",
  "negative_prompt": "Standard negatives + 'professional camera, DSLR, bokeh balls, anamorphic, cinema lighting, studio lighting'"
}
</json_schema>

<task>
Await user description of the scene. Generate the JSON output immediately.
</task>
```

## Hyper-Realistic Streetwear Portrait with Facial Preservation

```
An amateur street-style photo taken on an iPhone rear camera. Use the uploaded face as the ONLY and EXACT facial reference - preserve facial geometry, proportions, likeness and age 1:1. Do not alter facial features even by 1%. No beautification, no stylization. Framing & angle: medium shot, camera slightly above waist level, close to the subject, natural iPhone perspective, subject centered. Pose: model sitting on a low wooden platform, torso slightly leaning back, both hands resting behind on the surface for support, shoulders relaxed, head slightly tilted downward and turned to the side, calm grounded posture. Scene: urban skatepark or street-art zone, colorful graffiti walls, layered paint textures, metal structures in background, raw city atmosphere, street culture environment. Outfit: matte dark oversized T-shirt, loose black cargo pants, textured olive-green suede or matte canvas jacket, minimal belt detail, streetwear styling, visible stitching and fabric structure. Emotion: serious, introspective expression, quiet confidence, relaxed street attitude. Lighting: natural overcast daylight, soft diffused light, gentle shadows, no flash, cinematic but realistic contrast. Skin realism: hyper-realistic skin texture, visible pores, subtle imperfections, natural skin tone variation. Hair: natural hairline, individual strands visible, soft texture. Hands: natural skin texture, realistic shading across knuckles and fingers. Textures: worn graffiti wall surface, chipped paint, rough concrete floor, matte fabric fibers, jacket material texture. Camera look: authentic iPhone photo, sharp focus on subject, mild digital noise, natural color tones, unedited iPhone RAW feel, high realism, 3:4 format.
```

## Cinematic Seaside Scene Prompt with Detailed Texture Requirements

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Cinematic seaside scene at sunset with a dramatic, cloud-filled sky. The palette blends cool blue-gray atmospheric tones with a soft peach glow near the horizon. The person sits sideways on a pebbled beach close to the shoreline, body oriented toward the sea, gaze directed over the water. Posture natural and relaxed, anatomically correct, no exaggerated posing. A light coastal breeze creates subtle environmental movement — gentle fabric motion and natural interaction with the surroundings. Seagulls circle in the sky, some with slight motion blur for realistic depth and spatial layering. Wardrobe is minimal and neutral: a dark basic T-shirt layered under a loose knitted cardigan, paired with relaxed straight-leg jeans. Fabrics must show realistic textile behavior — visible knit texture, natural folds, weight, gravity tension at elbows and waist, authentic denim structure and stitching details. Lighting is soft natural sunset light with realistic falloff. Subtle rim light from the horizon, balanced exposure on skin without over-smoothing. Shadows remain matte and dimensional. Shot with a photographer’s mindset — 35mm lens look, natural perspective, shallow but believable depth of field. Vintage film-inspired color science reminiscent of Kodak Portra 400: soft contrast, muted highlights, matte shadows, mild desaturation, organic film grain. Editorial framing, high photorealism, 4K resolution.
```

## Detailed Japanese Prompt for Variety Show Style Image

Please output 4 times using the following prompt:

Text
Main: {argument name="main text" default="Miracle at Broadcast Accident Level"}
Sub: {argument name="sub text" default="Funny Cat Mishap Collection"} (bottom banner)
Other: Why did that happen w (speech bubble), Decisive Moment (top right stamp)

Color Scheme
Background: General living room (with signs of life, no specific brand furniture).
Text: Pop colors like #FFA500 (orange) and #FFFF00 (yellow).
Accent: Colors for comic-style concentration lines.

Font
Main: Fun, extra-bold font typical of variety shows.
Sub: Friendly, rounded text.

Layout
Text Placement:
Large main title at the top. Add movement to the text (italics, curve).
Place 'w' or speech bubbles near the cat's face.
Decoration/Figures:
Main subject is a cat (or dog).
Moments like failing a jump and being in a strange posture, funny faces, or flipping things over.
Motion blur to emphasize movement (subject blur) and comic sound effect effects (Don!, Biyooon, etc.).
Show the messy room without hiding it.

Style
Pet video, mishap, comedy, decisive moment, sense of dynamism, friendly, smartphone photo style
```

The mood is dreamy and romantic, with a touch of melancholy, evoking a sense of springtime and feminine beauty. The left arm is extended, with the palm resting firmly on the chair's frame, showcasing a relaxed but stylish body language typical of early 2000s party photography.

### **Photography: Low-Fidelity Digital Realism**
The image utilizes the distinct visual language of a mid-2000s mobile camera. It features a heavy, authentic digital grain and a direct, harsh flash that creates a high-contrast look, bleaching the central subjects while casting deep, sharp shadows behind them. The background is a warm, unfocused blur of a golden chandelier and ambient indoor lighting, providing a rich, textured depth.

*  **The Garment:** The subject wears a stylish, high-coverage cream-colored athletic bodysuit. The fabric is a fine-ribbed jersey cotton, featuring a delicate scalloped lace trim along the neckline and short sleeves, blending sportswear comfort with chic Y2K detailing.

The left bun is wrapped in a voluminous white vintage lace scrunchie, while the right bun is adorned with a crisp white silk ribbon tied into a structural bow. Wispy, face-framing strands are left out to soften the look.
*  **Ink & Ornamentation:** Meticulous fine-line tattoos are visible on the uncovered skin of the arms and upper chest. These include a detailed monarch butterfly on the deltoid, a minimalist heart on the inner bicep, and a centered gothic-style emblem on the upper chest.
*  **Grooming & Makeup:** The aesthetic is defined by "igari-style" blush—a heavy wash of vibrant pink pigment extending across the cheekbones and the bridge of the nose. Her gaze is directed straight at the camera, accompanied by a gentle smile and flawless makeup: well-defined eyebrows, voluminous false eyelashes, and an intense red lipstick that harmonizes with her crimson lace lingerie and high-waisted garter belt.

Plush Monkey Details: Giant IKEA Djungelskog Orangutan stuffed orangutan (viral model), positioned on the left, dominating the frame.
Extremely soft, dense, and fluffy fur in warm rust-brown and chocolate-brown tones, with rich, voluminous texture and individual fibers catching the light.
The muzzle/face/ears/chest area features a lighter creamy-beige soft fleece.
Large rounded head, rounded embroidered black eyes, small dark embroidered nose, and a mouth with a soft, friendly expression.
Long, flexible arms and legs, with a highly huggable and cozy appearance.

Visual Elements and Atmosphere
Lighting and Setting: The background consists of a simple light-colored wall, brought to life by dramatic warm spotlights that project the silhouette of a heart just behind the pair, creating soft and cozy shadows.
Composition: The camera, positioned at a slightly low eye-level angle, uses a shallow depth of field to keep full focus on the woman and the plush monkey, leaving the background softly blurred.
Color Palette: Vibrant reds, earthy browns, and creamy hues dominate, reinforcing the warmth and coziness of the composition. Image size: 3:4."
```

## 90s Movie Baddie Retro Portrait Prompt

```
Create a retro, vintage-inspired image - grainy yet bright with little sun rays based on the reference picture. The boy should be wearing a light maroon shirt, paired with plated off-white pants styled in a Pinterest-inspired aesthetic. The vibe must capture the essence of a 90s movie dark-brown-haired baddie, hair waving by a windy, romantic atmosphere. Standing under the aesthetic street light and reading a book as a few leaves blow in the air, while dramatic contrasts add mystery and artistry to the scene, creating a moody yet enchanting atmosphere.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Portrait with Oni Mask and Cinematic Lighting

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the face in the photo. Create an image. Ultra-detailed. Ultra-realistic.

Scene & Atmosphere:
A dramatic, close-up portrait set against a smoky, dusk-pink background swirling with soft, fog-like textures. Ambient light casts moody shadows across the scene, blending warm red undertones with cool charcoal gradients. There is a subtle backlight that catches the edges of her hair, giving a cinematic halo effect.

Pose & Expression:
The woman turns slightly over her shoulder, her face half-hidden behind a dark, intricately carved oni mask she holds close. One eye remains fully visible — sharpened, intense, and subtly glowing with warm, reflective catchlight. Her expression is calm but dangerously alluring, as if she’s revealing only a part of her true identity.

--
Hair (Important):
Long, jet-black layered hair flowing past the chest with soft internal layering for natural movement and dimension.
Full blunt bangs grazing just above the eyebrows, slightly feathered at the edges to avoid heaviness. The fringe is dense at the center and subtly tapered toward the temples for a refined, face-framing effect.
Hair texture is ultra-sleek with a healthy, glass-like shine, yet retains soft movement at the ends. Layers are seamless and blended — no choppy or uneven cuts.
Subtle natural lift at the crown adds quiet volume while maintaining a smooth silhouette.
Backlight catches the outer edges of the hair, creating a luminous halo outline against the smoky background.

---
Makeup (Important):
Flawless porcelain matte skin with soft inner glow on high points.
Skin texture smooth and refined, natural depth preserved.
Eyes: Gradient shadow technique — deep rose blending into muted plum and warm taupe.
Soft diffused blending, no harsh lines.
Lifted eyeliner wing with tightlined upper lash line.
Subtle aegyo-sal contour under the eyes for dimension.
Individual lash clusters for natural but defined volume.
Brows: Straight-to-soft-arched Korean brow shape, refined and elongated tail.
Lips: Muted deep rose gradient lip with softly diffused edges and subtle satin sheen.
Hazel eyes with reflective catchlight.

---
Oni Mask (Ultra-realistic):
A traditional Japanese oni mask made of matte obsidian-black lacquer.

Sharp, curved horns in bone-white ivory.

Intricate carvings along the cheekbones and forehead.

Thin cracks and subtle texture to show realism and craftsmanship.

Glowing ember-red eye slits and jaw lines that reflect off the woman’s skin, creating a fiery contrast.

Mask is positioned just enough to reveal her own eye — making the two faces coexist.

---
Outfit:
A sculpted asymmetrical black top crafted from matte stretch silk-crepe with subtle sheen under rim light.
One shoulder fully exposed, revealing defined collarbone and elegant neckline.
The opposite side features a structured, sculpted shoulder with clean architectural
```

(9:16). Raw iPhone 17 Pro, 24mm wide-angle, f/5.6 structural depth, sharp focus, digital grain. Identity Lock Reference A: absolute facial/hair geometry inheritance. Bio-Fidelity: micro-pores, satin-finish athletic hydration (sheen), epidermal physics on midriff and thighs. Expression Lock Reference B: lateral head angle toward mirror, V-sign hand gesture. Outfit: Black technical high-performance compression sports bra; olive-drab technical seamless rib-knit gym shorts with high-tensile compression and architectural waist-shaping. Pose: Seated on a technical leg press machine, lateral torso torsion, right hand articulated in a V-gesture with realistic finger physics. Lighting: Industrial overhead gym fluorescent lighting, sharp ray-traced highlights on muscular topography. Environment: Pixel-perfect gym interior, leg press machine footplate and rails, stacks of iron weight plates, weight benches, textured grey concrete floor, distant racks and equipment.

Intimate close-up portrait of a young Eurasian woman, early 20s,

LAYOUT: Vertical scroll landing page layout, full-bleed hero image.

POSE:

- Cheek softly resting on back of hand

- Body angled 3/4 toward camera

FACE STRUCTURE:
- Oval face with high sculpted cheekbones
- Sharp defined jawline, delicate pointed chin
- Straight refined nose with soft tip
- Full plump lips, glossy nude rose, softly parted in slight pout
SKIN:
- Flawless airbrushed porcelain complexion

- Perfectly smooth like retouched magazine cover
- Subtle natural luminosity, dewy glass-skin finish
- Even skin tone throughout

- Direct intense gaze into camera

- Well-defined arched eyebrows, darker than hair
HAIR:
- Long wavy brunette hair, silky texture
- Pulled back away from forehead, forehead fully visible
- Soft wispy face-framing pieces on sides
- Voluminous half-updo at back (not visible)

EXPRESSION:
- Sensual confident gaze
- Lips softly parted, subtle pout
- Quiet intensity, captivating

- Sparkling diamond stud earrings
- Stylish beige trench coat elegantly draped over her shoulders
- Creamy vanilla latte from a sleek ceramic cup with a minimalist logo

- Soft diffused light from front-left
- Subtle warm tones, golden hour

- Selfie angle from slightly above eye level with arm slightly extended
CAMERA: Shot on Hasselblad X2D 100C, 85mm f/1.4, eye level, intimate portrait distance
MOOD: Soft sensuality, elevated casual fashion, sophisticated vibe

TYPOGRAPHY: A stylish handwritten signature "{argument name="signature" default="Willy"}" is elegantly and small letters placed at the Bottom Right corner.

Style keywords: portrait photography, beauty shot, close-up, Eurasian model, Parisian cafe, luxury lifestyle, soft lighting, dewy skin, editorial fashion.

She exudes a serious and calm atmosphere with a focused expression, and her face is sharply in focus while people move quickly around her. Strong radial motion blur is applied around the subject, featuring a dynamic movement effect and shallow depth of field. Natural soft sunlight, subdued colors, soft beige and gray tones. Centered composition, conceptualizing emotional isolation, 85mm lens, f/1.8, ultra-detailed skin texture, realistic photography, high dynamic range, 8K resolution.
```

## Pixar Sticker Sheet Generation Prompt

> Yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a Pixar sticker sheet based on the person in the photo. The stickers should feature various expressive reactions in a cute, cartoon-like style, keeping the hairstyle, facial features, and outfit colors similar to the reference. Each sticker must have a different mood or gesture: Laughing Smiling sweetly Blushing with heart eyes (love) Angry pout Hand on chin (thinking) Crying with big teary eyes Shocked or surprised Waving hello Sparkling with joy Holding a dessert plate.
```

## Photorealistic Cinematic Portrait with Miniature Clones

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
'Use the uploaded reference image as the composition and pose reference.
Use my uploaded face photo as the ONLY face reference for the main character.

100% identity lock: same eyes, nose, lips, jawline, skin tone, hair texture. No change in ethnicity or facial structure. Real human photorealism, no Al plastic look.

Create a photorealistic cinematic portrait replicating the exact composition, pose, and surreal concept of the reference image. The main female subject is sitting cross-legged on a wooden floor with a warm smile.

She wears the identical red top puff sleeevs, denim jeans and yellow sneakers shoes . Surround him with seven miniature, realistic versions of himself (all sharing my face reference with slightly enlarged heads and playful expressions).

Two miniatures stand on her upturned palms, two sit on her shoulders, one pushes at her right shoe, one runs in the foreground, and one hugs her left knee from behind, matching the poses and placement from the reference image precisely.

Soft high-quality studio lighting creates realistic soft shadows. Background: clean gradient grey studio wall.

Ultra-sharp, highly detailed textures, cinematic depth, photorealistic lighting, 8K quality, natural skin texture, no CGI look.

Negative prompt: cartoon, 3D render, anime, chibi, painting, drawing, low quality, blurry, distorted face, bad anatomy, extra fingers, missing limbs, mismatched faces, different clothing colors, warped proportions, artifacts.

Aspect ratio: 4:5'
```

Accessories include fluffy white faux-fur wrist cuffs, red-and-white fluffy knee-high leg warmers, a thin-chain silver pendant necklace, and a silver belly button piercing.
She stands facing forward with hands on hips and elbows out, framed in a medium shot from knees to head, vertical 3:4. A dark bag or jacket lies on the floor to the bottom right, with a white radiator and black door handle/lock visible.
Lighting is direct on-camera flash with a harsh shadow cast on the door behind her, giving an early-2000s digital camera, candid night-out snapshot aesthetic. Dominant colors are red and white from the outfit, warm skin tones, and a neutral beige-and-white background.

## 8K Photorealistic Close-Up Selfie Prompt on Ornate Couch

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A photorealistic, intimate close-up selfie of a beautiful young woman lying comfortably on her back across an ornate, richly patterned couch in a cozy indoor home setting. The couch upholstery features intricate floral and baroque-inspired motifs in warm, muted tones, adding texture and visual depth to the background while still keeping the focus entirely on her face.

The camera angle is positioned directly above her, as if she’s holding the phone slightly raised over her head, creating a soft, flattering perspective. Her long, wavy brown hair fans out naturally around her head, spreading across the couch like a halo, with individual strands visible in fine detail. The waves catch the ambient indoor light, showing subtle variations in warm chestnut and caramel undertones.

She is wearing a loose lavender short-sleeve top with delicate horizontal stripes. The fabric looks soft and lightweight, slightly relaxed at the neckline, which falls naturally and casually without appearing posed. The gentle folds of the material create realistic shadows and highlights, enhancing the authenticity of the scene. A delicate, thin necklace rests against her collarbone, catching a soft glimmer of light and adding a subtle elegant touch.

Her face is the focal point—highly detailed and sharply rendered. Her striking bright turquoise-blue eyes look directly into the camera lens, creating an immediate sense of connection with the viewer. The eyes are glossy and reflective, with tiny light reflections visible in the irises, giving them depth and realism. Her lashes are natural yet defined, framing her gaze beautifully.

Her skin texture is realistic and finely detailed—soft, smooth, and luminous under gentle natural indoor lighting. The light appears to be coming from a nearby window, diffused and warm, casting soft highlights across her cheekbones, nose bridge, and forehead while maintaining gentle, flattering shadows along the contours of her face. Subtle variations in skin tone and faint natural imperfections enhance the photorealism.

Her full lips are slightly parted, coated with a subtle gloss that reflects light softly, adding dimension without appearing heavy. There’s a calm, relaxed sensuality in her expression—confident yet effortless. One arm is bent behind her head, lifting her shoulder slightly and creating a natural curve in her posture. The pose feels spontaneous rather than staged, intimate yet refined.

The overall composition emphasizes closeness and authenticity—an 8K ultra-detailed, cinematic-quality selfie with realistic skin texture, soft natural lighting, shallow depth of field, and a warm, inviting indoor atmosphere.

## Soft-Focus Ethereal Portrait with Motion Blur

> Sinematik, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A soft-focus, cinematic portrait of a young woman with delicate facial features and light golden hair, caught in motion. Her face appears partially blurred and ghosted, with flowing strands of hair captured mid-sway, creating a dreamy, ethereal motion effect. Her expression is serene and slightly distant, with parted lips and eyes softly open. The entire frame is bathed in golden beige and sepia tones, evoking a nostalgic, painterly atmosphere. The lighting is natural and diffused, coming from a nearby window, casting a warm glow on her skin and illuminating the strands of hair that dance across the frame. Photographed with a Canon EOS R5 using an 85mm lens at f/1.2, ISO 320, shutter speed 1/8s with intentional slow exposure and handheld movement to create the artistic motion blur. The focus is soft, with a shallow depth of field that isolates the subject from the pale, creamy background. Post-processed in Adobe Lightroom and Photoshop: cinematic LUTs applied, warmth enhanced through split-toning (shadows in soft amber, highlights in cream), clarity reduced, and motion streaks refined with displacement layers and radial blur filters. A very subtle fine grain adds a filmic texture. Final mood: emotional, hazy, poetic. Style references: fine art portraiture, vintage editorial, and analog dream sequences. 4K ultra-resolution, no harsh contrasts, smooth gradients preserved.
```

## High-End Thai Celebrity Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject
A young adult Thai woman in her 20s seated in a worn vintage brown leather armchair. She has sleek, glossy dark brown to jet-black hair styled in a smooth shoulder-length blowout or sharp blunt bob with a deep side part. Hair is ultra-shiny and perfectly styled. Her skin has a luminous warm-neutral undertone with a flawless celebrity glow. Her expression is poised, confident, and subtly alluring, maintaining soft but intentional direct eye contact with the camera.

Makeup & Styling
High-gloss Thai celebrity glam inspired by red-carpet Thai actresses and luxury beauty campaigns: perfected radiant glass-skin base with seamless coverage, softly sculpted brows with clean definition, precise espresso-brown eyeliner with a sharp lifted wing, subtly smoked outer corners, tightlined upper lash line, wispy volume lashes, champagne-gold inner corner highlight, sculpted yet natural contour, luminous golden highlighter on cheekbones and nose bridge, vibrant peach-coral blush blended upward toward the temples, and high-shine lacquered coral-rose or warm ruby gloss lips. Polished, refined, glamorous but not heavy.

Outfit
A silky ivory or champagne satin camisole or slip top with elegant draping, slightly off-shoulder styling. Optional delicate fine gold jewelry such as thin hoop earrings or a minimalist gold chain for understated luxury.

Pose
Seated confidently in the armchair, torso angled slightly to the side with shoulders relaxed but poised. One leg crossed elegantly. Chin slightly tilted down or to the side for a cinematic feel. Three-quarter body framing.

Photography Style
Soft but directional window light from the left enhanced with subtle studio-style fill for flawless skin rendering. Controlled highlights, smooth shadow transitions, ultra-clean finish, slight glow diffusion for a luxury editorial look. Eye-level or slightly above eye-level perspective. 9:16 vertical aspect ratio. Shallow depth of field with creamy background blur.

Background
Minimal upscale interior corner with a softly textured off-white wall and polished dark wood or warm teak flooring. Clean, refined, modern Thai luxury ambiance.

Mood & Vibe
Elegant, radiant, high-end Thai celebrity aura. Glossy, confident, refined femininity. Cinematic polish with warmth. No harsh shadows, no over-contouring, no messy styling, no text or watermark.
```

## Streetwear Couple Film Shoot Prompt (Empty Pool)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded photos as the ONLY appearance standards: keep the faces of both people 1:1 (features, proportions, age, skin tone, hair color/length), without changing their appearance even by 1%, without enhancing, without facial retouching. Ultra-realistic film fashion shoot of the couple (35mm film photo), streetwear aesthetics, "caught on film". Location: an empty concrete pool/skate pool with smooth curves (empty pool bowl), worn paint in blue and mint shades, signs of use and light dirt are visible, night-time atmosphere. Frame: vertical 3:4, tilted composition (slight Dutch angle), medium shot. The couple is positioned diagonally in the frame. Pose (important): the guy is sitting by the wall of the pool, his back leaning against the edge and looking at the camera. The girl is lying on top of the edge, her head hanging down next to the guy's head (their heads almost touching). The girl's hair hangs loosely down. The mood is calm, slightly detached. Images: identical white oversized streetwear sweatshirts with large black text and a red graphic element on the chest (no specific brands/logos), identical neutral light-colored shorts/bottoms, minimalism. Lighting: hard flash to the forehead (direct on-camera flash), strong shadows and sharp contrast, "snapshot" aesthetic, bright highlights on the skin and fabric, darker background. Film style: real film grain, slight color shift, light green/blue cast, soft blur at the edges, subtle vignette, dust/scratches, analog sharpness. Realism: genuine leather (pore detail), natural hair, textured sweatshirt fabric. Aspect ratio 3:4.
```

## Seamless Cinematic Japanese Food Video Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Generate a cinematic video focused on Japanese cuisine, consisting of four seamlessly connected cuts.

The first cut should be a close-up, carefully capturing cooking sounds, the rhythm of the knife, and the movement of hands touching the ingredients, starting with ASMR-like sound design that emphasizes steam and ingredient textures. The camera should use shallow depth of field and slow motion to naturally guide the viewer from the hands to the woman's gestures, showing her cuteness through small movements like subtle surprise or a gentle smile. Transition smoothly from here using match cuts or pans.

The second cut is a mid-shot of plating, beautifully capturing the movement of chopsticks and the moment ingredients are placed on the dish, emphasizing the delicate sounds of the dish and ingredients resonating, highlighting charm through carefulness and affection.

The subsequent cut uses an overhead composition to show the overall view of the finished dish, with the camera slowly zooming in, capturing the moment the woman takes a bite in a close-up, delicately capturing her breathing and subtle reactions to invite viewer empathy.

The final part connects to a warm interaction across the table, using a soft pan and crossfade to leave a lingering impression, subtly mixing laughter and fragments of conversation to express intimacy. The color tone should be filmic and warm-leaning, with natural lighting that keeps highlights gently suppressed. The music should be minimalist piano with Japanese instrument accents, and sound effects should carefully mix cooking and eating sounds to enhance realism. The overall tempo should be relaxed, prioritizing the natural connection of each cut's end to the next beginning through sound and movement.
```

## Photorealistic Highland Lake Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-detailed photorealistic selfie of a beautiful young woman with delicate feminine features, long straight light brown hair flowing smoothly over her shoulders, and clear blue eyes softly reflecting natural daylight. She has subtle, natural makeup — light foundation with visible realistic skin texture, soft peach blush, lightly glossed lips, gently defined brows, and a natural glow. A soft, relaxed smile enhances her warm and approachable expression.

She is holding the camera at arm’s length in true iPhone-style selfie perspective, creating a slightly close, intimate framing with mild wide-angle lens distortion typical of a smartphone front camera. Her posture is casual yet elegant, shoulders relaxed, head slightly tilted toward the light. One arm is extended toward the camera while the other rests naturally near her waist.

She wears an elegant sleeveless white V-neck jumpsuit/dress featuring a deep plunge neckline and refined button details running vertically down the front. The fabric appears lightweight yet structured, softly contouring her silhouette. A matching belt cinches at the waist, emphasizing her figure. The material catches the sunlight with gentle highlights and subtle shadows, revealing realistic folds and texture.

Accessories include statement crystal drop earrings that sparkle in the sunlight, a delicate gold necklace with a small pendant resting just above her neckline, multiple thin bracelets layered on her wrist, and a fine chain bracelet adding a subtle shimmer. The jewelry reflects natural light with crisp, high-detail clarity.

The setting is a scenic highland lake during a warm sunny day. A wooden railing appears along the side of the frame, adding depth and context to the composition. In the foreground, lush green reeds and marsh grasses frame the bottom edge of the image. Behind her, calm reflective lake water mirrors the sky, while distant reddish-brown mountains and rolling hills stretch across the horizon. The sky is partly cloudy with soft white clouds drifting across a vibrant blue background.

Natural outdoor portrait photography style with cinematic lighting, balanced exposure, vibrant yet realistic colors, sharp focus on the subject’s face, softly blurred background (subtle depth of field), high dynamic range, ultra-high resolution 8k detail, crisp skin texture, realistic hair strands, natural shadows, and warm sunny atmosphere. Professional mobile photography aesthetic, hyper-realistic, immersive, detailed environmental storytelling.
```

## Mixed Media Portrait with Chibi Overlays

A high quality mixed media portrait of a trendy young man (using my uploaded person photo) standing on a train station platform at night. The subject is posing coolly, holding the strap of an olive backpack with one hand. He is wearing a stylish streetwear outfit with an oversized layered black hoodie, gray sweatpants, and chunky sneakers.

The background features a realistic KRL Jabodetabek Commuter Line train side view with its signature red striping, with the train doors wide open, bathed in bright cinematic station lighting and city night ambiance.

Surrounding the realistic main subject are several cute 3D style chibi versions of himself, same outfit and same face. These chibis are interacting playfully, one sitting on his shoulder, one climbing his leg, and another peeking out from the backpack.

Overlay the image with vibrant hand drawn doodle effects, white drawn outlines around the subject, playful scribbles, stars, sparkles, electric sparks, and floating white and black handwritten slogans like “forever young”, “krl”, “night”, and “gen z vibes”. The style should seamlessly blend hyper realistic photography with colorful flat cartoon illustrations while keeping the face and body shape unchanged.
```

## Boho Editorial Portrait in Pine Forest

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Young woman with extremely long, sleek straight blonde hair cascading like liquid silk down her back, individual strands catching the light with soft golden highlights, captured in a graceful side-profile pose that emphasizes her delicate features and natural jawline. She wears a gentle, effortless smile — subtle, warm, and serene — eyes softly focused ahead as if lost in a peaceful moment within nature. Her posture is relaxed yet elegant, shoulders slightly turned, creating a refined feminine silhouette.

She stands surrounded by a dense forest of towering pine trees, their tall vertical trunks forming a majestic natural cathedral around her. The forest floor is textured with pine needles, soft moss, and scattered cones, adding rich earthy detail. Light filters down through high branches in soft golden rays, creating dreamy beams and dappled highlights across her hair, skin, and outfit. The atmosphere feels fresh and quiet, with a slight mist in the distance adding depth and cinematic layering.

She wears a handcrafted bohemian crochet top featuring intricate geometric patterns in warm beige, muted brown, and soft ivory white tones. The knit texture is highly detailed, showcasing delicate stitching, openwork designs, and artisanal craftsmanship. The off-shoulder neckline gently reveals her collarbones and upper shoulders, enhancing the romantic boho aesthetic. Dramatic bell sleeves flow loosely from her arms, adorned with airy cutout holes that allow glimpses of skin and create beautiful shadow patterns in the sunlight.

Her brown layered tulle skirt falls gracefully from the waist, featuring multiple soft translucent layers that create dimension and gentle movement. The fabric appears light and airy, slightly lifted by a soft forest breeze, adding a sense of motion and natural elegance. Around her neck rests a subtle layered necklace — fine chains with small organic pendants — complementing the earthy color palette. A natural light urban portrait of a young man standing beside a textured brick wall on a city street. He is shown in a soft side profile, looking slightly away from the camera with a calm and thoughtful expression. Short textured brown hair with a neat fade on the sides, natural skin texture with light freckles, strong eyebrows, and soft neutral lips. He wears a dark denim jacket layered over a simple grey t shirt, casual modern style.
Shot in a shallow depth of field with strong background blur, creating smooth bokeh from an out of focus city street and buildings behind him. Warm daylight falls gently across the face, producing soft highlights and natural shadows. Realistic color grading, cinematic yet natural tones, sharp facial details, editorial lifestyle photography style, 85mm lens look, eye level angle, ultra realistic, high resolution, authentic street portrait mood.
```

## Candid Bedroom Selfie of Three Freckled Girls

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
three freckled European girls in an intimate close-up selfie, low-angle perspective with their faces very close together, heads gently touching as they lean toward the smartphone camera, creating a natural slight wide-angle lens distortion that softly exaggerates their features in a playful, candid way. All three have long flowing hair in different shades—golden blonde, light chestnut brown, and soft dark blonde—falling naturally around their shoulders with a few loose strands catching the warm indoor light. Their skin shows realistic texture with visible freckles scattered across their cheeks and noses, subtle pores, soft blush tones, and a healthy natural glow without heavy makeup.

They wear simple casual tank tops in soft neutral tones like white, beige, and pastel pink, paired with delicate minimal jewelry—thin gold chains, tiny pendant necklaces, small hoop earrings, and subtle rings that reflect the ambient light. Their expressions are relaxed and genuine, cute natural smiles with slightly squinted eyes, conveying a friendly, spontaneous bedroom selfie moment.

The background shows a cozy bedroom setting slightly out of focus—soft bedding, light-colored walls, maybe a blurred mirror or fairy lights—enhanced by warm indoor lighting that creates gentle shadows and depth. The image feels like it was shot on a modern smartphone, ultra-detailed yet natural, realistic skin texture with freckles and pores clearly visible, candid friendly mood, soft shadows, warm color grading, high-resolution, authentic lifestyle photography style.
```

## Neo-Skeuomorphism Crowd Scene Prompt

> Illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A close-up, overhead shot shows a crowd of abstract, cartoonish figures with large, expressive eyes. The figures are rendered in a variety of bold colors, including a bright blue starfish-like creature, a fuzzy black blob with a triangular nose, a vibrant orange star-shaped being, and a lime green amorphous shape. These characters are packed tightly together, creating a sense of a dense, bustling community. The background is a dark, textured black, which makes the colorful figures pop. The overall style is whimsical and slightly unsettling, with a textured, crayon-like aesthetic.
```

## Flash-Lit Jester-Girl Editorial Anime

> Minimal, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
TYPE:
[Flash-Lit Close-up / Jester-Girl Performance Editorial Anime — lowkey sensual]
FORMAT:
[3:4 (vertical portrait), Single Image Only]
IDENTITY LOCK:
[Same series character design language: 90s shōjo anime proportions and art style, clean linework, consistent face style. Character: Jester performer, two-toned harlequin makeup, stylized soft-belled hood. Adult, hourglass-voluptuous silhouette (curved waist-to-hip silhouette, powerful thighs, soft fullness balanced by muscle). No real-person likeness.]
CAMERA:
[35mm equivalent, close distance, tight crop; subject fills 75–85% of frame; subtle wide-angle edge distortion that accentuates curves; shallow depth of field; frozen motion at peak dance moment; no overlays.]
LIGHTING:
[Harsh direct on-camera flash; crisp hard shadow carving contours against stone; specular blowouts on chrome bells and marble floor; background falls into deep gloom; torch sconces rendered only as warm, blurred bokeh circles.]
MATERIAL STACK:
Base: [High-cut glossy harlequin-pattern technical performance fabric hugging curves; polished chrome jester bells; black-and-white checkered marble floor; heavy velvet curtains in background]
Coating: [High-spec clearcoat sheen on costume seams + chrome; thin condensation sheen on skin-safe fabric surfaces]
Wear: [Micro-scratches on chrome bells; scuff marks on marble; gentle fabric stretch lines at hips/waist; worn texture on ancient stone pillars]
Contamination: [Fine dust motes floating in stagnant castle air catching flash; faint sensor grain; subtle atmospheric haze]
SCALE ANCHORS:
[Jester bells clearly visible; costume stitching detail; blurred throne elements or stone arches as bokeh; shallow DOF falloff behind subject]
MICROFLAWS:
[Flash bloom halos on highlights; slight chromatic aberration on brightest edges; tiny compression grain; clean but tactile]
NOVELTY:
[Anachronistic contrast: High-gloss anime aesthetics vs. ancient medieval court texture; voyeuristic editorial energy (no UI/text); suggestive tension through pose and lighting]
ACTION:
[Peak acrobatic dance movement, physically plausible; dramatic arched torso, fluid limb extension, playful yet confident gaze; motion frozen by flash against the heavy stone backdrop]
```

## Candid Night Street Fashion Photograph

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
High-angle candid night street fashion photograph, shot from an upper floor across the street, looking downward.
A young woman (face photo reference) stands alone near a minimarket entrance, slightly off-center. Full body visible with open negative space.
She wears a white T-shirt, a washed blue denim jacket, dark straight-leg jeans, and white canvas sneakers. A small tote bag hangs from one shoulder. Hair loose or tied low, slightly messy.
Bright minimarket lighting spills onto the pavement, creating soft reflections. Glass doors show blurred interior shelves and posters.
She stands still for a moment, holding a white takeaway coffee cup while the other hand adjusts her tote bag strap.
She glances upward casually, unaware of the camera.
Cool fluorescent light mixed with warm street lighting.
Shot on Canon AE-1 film, candid street editorial mood.
Slight desaturation, fine film grain.
Sharp subject, gently softened background.
No other people. No readable text.
Aspect ratio 4:5.
```

## OOTD Sticker Style Breakdown

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Mia OOTD Stickers Using my uploaded selfie as the exact reference, keep my face, body, proportions, and outfit completely unchanged with photorealistic accuracy.
Add white sticker-style borders around individual outfit items, as if they were cut out and pasted onto the image. Include aesthetic fashion notebook annotations, with soft pencil-style handwritten labels and arrows pointing to each item.
The annotations should feel like a scrapbook or fashion journal, minimal and clean, not overcrowded. Maintain the realism of the original photo while layering editorial-style outfit callouts on top.
Overall style feels like a Japanese/Korean fashion magazine OOTD breakdown, blending realistic photography with playful scrapbook elements, clean composition, soft tones, and a polished yet casual aesthetic.
```

## Surreal Black and White Close-Up Portrait with Luminous Lines

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyperrealistic 8K black and white extreme close-up portrait of a young man's face, focusing tightly on his eye, cheekbone, and lips. Bright sunlight breaks into soft, wavy luminous lines that ripple across his skin like underwater reflections. The light flows diagonally, wrapping around the contours of his face in surreal patterns, while the shadows dissolve into smooth mid-gray tones instead of deep black. The skin is rendered in lifelike detail-visible pores, faint freckles, subtle stubble, and natural imperfections catch the glowing light realistically. The lips are softly highlighted, and the eye reflects fragments of the luminous waves, adding depth and emotion without artificial glow. The camera framing feels macro, pressed close to the face, with razor-thin depth of field: one illuminated ridge of skin is perfectly sharp, while the rest melts into velvety blur. The glassy, fluid quality of the light lines gives the portrait a surreal dreamlike aesthetic while remaining grounded in photographic realism. The background disappears into soft gray gradients, leaving only the interplay of skin texture and luminous wave-like rays. Fine cinematic grain overlays the frame, enhancing the tactile analog feel. The overall mood is intimate, surreal, and emotionally evocative - a monochrome portrait.
```
